## Acceptance Criteria

*Ids are `AC-AA-n`. Rule ids are `AA-n` (Detailed Design), formulas `D.n`, edge cases `E-AA-n`, lints `L-n` (AA-47), Visual/Audio rows `AAV-n`; a neighbour's id is written with its prefix (`AC-AT-47`, `AC-HE-71`, `LSG:1110`). Section A to I are the QA-lead-reviewed first set; section J holds the criteria the review added for rules, formulas and edge cases that the first set left open, and section K holds the Visual/Audio sync and policy criteria. Ids are not renumbered, so a reader holding the review can follow every change.*

**Tags.** `(auto)` a headless test with the dummy audio driver that asserts **cue requests**, which are pure functions of published state (AA-3); it lives in `tests/unit/audio/`, or in `tests/integration/audio/` when tagged `integration`. `(lint)` a build-time, data-load or source lint, run in the same CI job as the unit tests. `(review)` a document or discharge review that produces a checklist, not a build artefact. `(manual)` a human listening test with a **retained capture**: the file in `production/qa/evidence/` carries the criterion id and the date in its name, plus a result sheet (tester count, trials, outcome); a capture that was made and discarded is an assertion, not evidence. `(spike)` a measurement whose recorded result decides an ADR; it closes with the result on disk, not with a pass or a fail, except where a threshold is stated. Modifiers: `provisional PF-20` (the criterion depends on the Perception Filter's request transport, which is on disk and headed `Designed` but whose Audio rows are re-checked at the X-6 confirming scan), `provisional <neighbour>` (a read or an event that the owner has not yet confirmed), `VS-B dormant` (contract only at MVP, excluded from the MVP gate), `Alpha` (the surface does not exist at MVP). Every audibility claim is split into a headless **trigger** test and a manual **audible** test; the first proves the request exists, the second proves it can be heard.

**Value convention.** A criterion names a registry constant and gives its value "at time of writing" in parentheses at first use. The test fixture reads the registry or `assets/data/audio/tell_audio.json` as the source of truth, so a retune cannot leave a stale number in a test; restating the same value here is allowed, a different value is a CONFLICT. Level comparisons use a tolerance of 0.01 dB and the two-decimal values of the Formulas section; pitch comparisons use 0.1 cent; tick comparisons are exact.

**Manual-test panel protocol (applies to every `(manual)` criterion).** The solo developer knows every sound and cannot be the only panellist. A closing panel is at least 5 testers who have not seen the asset list, none of them the audio author; every blind identification pools at least 50 trials across the panel and a percentage threshold applies to the pooled trials; a count threshold ("at least 4 of 5 testers") applies per tester. A fallback panel of 3 is allowed at a pre-alpha checkpoint only and is marked "indicative, not closing" in the result sheet. Loudness is measured as K-weighted momentary loudness (400 ms) on the retained capture, band levels as band-limited RMS of the same capture. Whether a panel of this size is feasible for a solo first-time developer is an open question (OQ-AA-13).

**Evidence classes.** Logic and integration criteria are BLOCKING at the workflow's `full` tier. Every `(manual)` listening criterion is a Feel-class check whose retained capture lives in `production/qa/evidence/` and is not waived at `qa.level: minimal`; whether an audio capture counts as the Visual/Feel evidence that the testing standard words in terms of screenshots is an open question (OQ-AA-14). Config and data checks are advisory smoke checks except the load-time refusals listed here as lints, which block the boot.

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Sign-off (answers OQ-AA-14).** An audio capture with its result sheet counts as the Visual/Feel evidence of the testing standard; the audio-director signs it off, and the creative-director is the fallback when the audio-director is the author.

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Infrastructure prerequisites (Sprint-0 story S0-AUD-HARNESS).** No criterion below is schedulable until this story closes: (1) a **request-capture harness** that runs `cue_requests` headless against the dummy audio driver and returns the request list per poll; (2) a **lint runner** for the identity table, the asset directory, the data relations (D.8, D.10, D.19) and the source scans (L-1 to L-16); (3) a **draw-order rig** that stamps the tick and frame of the audio request and of the first visual draw (it is also the evidence rig of the Visual/Audio section); (4) a **reference machine** defined on disk (CPU, GPU, audio device, driver, project mix rate 48000, display refresh) for every spike and every p95 figure *(X14: the user has not yet named the minimum-spec machine, so none is named here and the figures wait for it)*; (5) the **gdUnit4** runner of the CI command in the coding standards, with a per-job runtime budget (the PR job within 120 s, the nightly job unbounded). Spikes AC-AA-6, AC-AA-80 and AC-AA-81 are measured on (4).

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Trial design (every blind test).** (i) Forced choice with the chance level stated (50 per cent for two items, 25 per cent for four); (ii) at least 10 trials per tester per condition, the order drawn once from a committed seed and counterbalanced by a Latin square over conditions; (iii) clips normalised to the same K-weighted loudness unless loudness is the variable; (iv) a percentage threshold is the pooled correct fraction, and the result must also clear chance by a one-sided binomial test at 95 per cent; (v) no feedback during a run; (vi) the playback device (headphones or laptop speakers) is recorded and the criterion names which one it binds; (vii) a tester who has seen the asset list, or who authored an asset, is excluded. **Panel criteria are indicative until the Vertical Slice**: closing evidence needs the full panel (OQ-AA-13). P2 Suit-cue blind tests (AC-AA-102) are excluded from the MVP gate while those cues are placeholders.

*(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **Slice map (every criterion carries one slice).** *MVP, blocking at `full`:* every `(auto)` and `(lint)` criterion of sections A to K except those named below. *MVP, indicative until the Vertical Slice:* every `(manual)` criterion (AC-AA-4, 4b, 8b, 12, 14, 27, 28, 36, 41, 52, 58, 59, 102, 119, 120, 121, 124, 126, 128, 138, 143). *MVP spikes (close on a recorded result):* AC-AA-6, 80, 81, 135. *Vertical Slice:* the spectral half of AC-AA-78, the asset half of AC-AA-53, AC-AA-102 with real Suit assets. *VS-B dormant:* AC-AA-62, 116. *Alpha:* AC-AA-75, 76, 123.

### A. Cue delivery, the tell and the abort (P0)

- **AC-AA-1 (auto)** GIVEN the 40-row fixture `tests/unit/audio/fixtures/d1_classifier_rows.json` (columns `seen_e, seen_b, seen_ph, now_e, now_b, now_ph, out, restore, expected_event`; the rows cover new epoch, same epoch higher beat, same epoch same beat, TELL to CALM with ABORTED, TELL to BREACH, CALM to BREACH, BREACH to SUBSIDING, SUBSIDING to CALM, a restore, a hitch that skips beats, and a breach with a stale higher beat), WHEN each row is polled, THEN the produced event equals `expected_event` (none or exactly one of ONSET, BEAT, RELEASE, BREACH_ENTER, SUBSIDE), no row produces two events (the D.1 rows are mutually exclusive on the published state), and `seen == now` after every poll. [AA-4, D.1, AUD-3]
- **AC-AA-2 (auto)** GIVEN `tell_epoch` 7 entering TELL at `T` 12000 and a 3-second hitch that publishes `(7, 2)` and `(7, 3)`, WHEN the next poll runs, THEN exactly one BEAT request (for beat 3) is made, beat 2 is never requested later, and `seen == (7, 3)`; GIVEN `seen == (7, 9)` and a poll of `(8, 0)`, THEN ONSET is requested. [AA-4, D.1, AC-AT-115]
- **AC-AA-3a (auto)** GIVEN the closed integer grid that Attention's tell-window fixture publishes (`tell_len` from `TELL_MIN_TICKS` (600) to `TELL_MAX_TICKS` (1800) in steps of 6 ticks, crossed with 32 epoch seeds at `BEAT_JITTER_TICKS` (8, nominal) and at jitter 0; the fixture is copied from Attention's `tell_window_contract` test and re-read at the X-6 scan), WHEN a tell is published and not aborted and the poll runs at 1-tick intervals, THEN the BEAT-plus-ONSET request count in the first `TELL_MIN_TICKS` is at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` (4) and beat 0 is the onset (no second beat-0 request). [AA-14, AA-15, AUD-5] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* **PR subset:** every 60th `tell_len` (21 values) x 4 epoch seeds x both jitters (168 tells), within a 10 s runtime budget in the PR job; the full grid runs nightly.
- **AC-AA-3b (auto)** GIVEN the same grid and polls at 1-tick intervals, WHEN every published beat is counted, THEN the number of BEAT requests equals the number of published beats after beat 0 and none is suppressed before `breach_due_T`. [AA-15, AUD-3]
- **AC-AA-4 (manual)** GIVEN a 600-tick armed tell captured audio-only at nominal frame rate with the screen hidden, WHEN each tester is asked the open question "what is happening to the ship?" and then asked to count the knocks, THEN every tester counts at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` (4) knocks and at least 4 of 5 testers describe the event as structural or hull-borne rather than as a machine or a monster; the audio-only capture and result sheet are retained (the audible half of AC-AT-47). [AA-15, AC-AT-47]
- **AC-AA-4b (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Run-gait variant. GIVEN a 600-tick armed tell captured audio-only while the avatar RUNS throughout (run footfalls at `FOOT_REF_DB + FOOT_RUN_DELTA_DB`, -3 dBr), at nominal frame rate, WHEN each tester counts the knocks, THEN every tester counts at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` (4) and at least 4 of 5 describe them as structural; headphones and laptop speakers are separate trials; the capture and result sheet are retained. [AA-15, AA-31, D.19 R-d]
- **AC-AA-5 (auto, integration)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN an onset and a presenter stub, WHEN the frame in which the `attention.step` run publishes beat 0 is processed (with any number of sim steps and notification-handler calls in that frame), THEN the handlers create no request, the frame poll creates the onset request in that same frame, and the stub draws the first filament for the epoch only after the request exists (a call-order guarantee, not a latency). [AA-4, AA-13, AA-14, AUD-2, AC-AT-24]
- **AC-AA-6 (spike)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the shipped audio stack on the defined reference machine, WHEN the audio-versus-screen delta (a loopback capture of the audio output beside a capture of the screen on one clock) is measured over 200 events each of beat 0 against the first filament, a witness class against its trail, and `strike_T` against the strike pose, THEN the p95 deltas are recorded in `production/qa/evidence/`, `AUDIO_LEAD_MS` is set from them if any p95 delta is negative, and OQ-AA-4 and OQ-AA-37 are answered; the absolute `play()`-to-DAC delay is not claimed, because the engine cannot observe it, and no rule changes without the result. [AA-13, HE:1285]
- **AC-AA-7 (auto)** GIVEN a tell whose published `tier` is driven by a fixture through 1, 2 and 3 (`TIER2_TICKS` 600 and `TIER3_TICKS` 1200 on Attention's escalation clock when it runs) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*, WHEN each tier is entered, THEN the bed layers change only at the tier step (crossfade `TIER_XFADE_TICKS`, `TIER_STEP_DB` 2 dB per step), the positional hull-stress layer is requested at tier 2 and 3 at the `hull_anchor` of `locus_section_id` and not at tier 1, and no request parameter varies continuously with `tell_elapsed` or the escalation clock; GIVEN a fixture in which `tier` stays 1 for 900 ticks (the escalation frozen, X4), THEN no tier-2 or tier-3 layer and no hull-stress request is made in that window. [AA-17, AA-18] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-AA-8a (auto + manual, headphones path)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the worst allowed concurrent state (`RUN_MAX_VOICES` (4) running loops at `RUN_CEIL_DB`, a TOOL-class loop, the ambience, the Suit fan, the berth bed, the INTEGRITY room tone (NORMAL band) and `WIT_VOICE_CAP` sustained witness classes), WHEN the tier-1 bed alone and the concurrent set alone are rendered through the shipped bus graph to a digital loopback capture (no loudspeaker, no microphone) and analysed as band-limited RMS over the two bed bands (about 30 to 120 Hz and 200 to 400 Hz), THEN the bed exceeds the set by at least `TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB` (6) in both bands, and the beats alone remain countable with the bed removed (the panel and count of AC-AA-4); the capture, the two band levels and the count are retained. [AA-16, D.6, M2]
- **AC-AA-8b (manual, laptop speakers)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the same scene played through laptop speakers, WHEN each tester is asked whether they can hear the hull bed under the machinery and then counts the knocks with the bed removed, THEN at least 4 of 5 testers report the bed audible and every tester counts at least `MIN_BEATS_IN_FLOOR` knocks; a band-level proof through a room is not claimed (it cannot be made with a microphone and a small speaker), so this criterion is perceptual; the recording and result sheet are retained. [AA-16, AA-45, M2]
- **AC-AA-9 (lint)** GIVEN the knob set, WHEN the data load computes `L_nc,b` for the worst concurrent set, THEN `BED_BASE_DB - L_nc,b >= TELL_AUDIBILITY_MARGIN_DB` for both bed bands (the D.6 example gives 8.16 dB at tier 1 and 12.16 dB at tier 3, with the berth bed and the INTEGRITY room tone (-44) in the set) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; a `BED_BASE_DB` of -24 gives 2.16 dB and the load is refused; a POWER hum authored at 100 Hz in `B_LOW` at -30 dBr gives 6.66 dB (passes the margin) and fails the band lint because its fundamental is below `POWER_HUM_MIN_HZ` (160). [D.6, AA-45]

- **AC-AA-65 (auto)** GIVEN a full pool, WHEN a tier-1 request finds only tier-1 victims, THEN it steals the quietest non-PROTECTED tier-1 voice quieter than itself and `voice_tier1_steal` increments; the same counter is non-zero in a CI soak only on a bug. [AA-12, D.10]
- **AC-AA-66 (lint)** GIVEN the data file, WHEN loaded, THEN `VOICE_POOL_SIZE >= RESERVED_TELL_IMPULSE_VOICES + RUN_MAX_VOICES + WIT_VOICE_CAP` (a pool of 12 is refused), `RESERVED_TELL_IMPULSE_VOICES >= max(1, ceil(BEAT_TAIL_MAX_TICKS / BEAT_MIN_GAP_TICKS))` (2 at a 60-tick tail and a 30-tick gap), and a warning is raised if the pool is below `D123` (31 at the defaults). [D.10, checkpoint Q3]
- **AC-AA-67 (auto)** GIVEN the tell bed, the tier stems, the reserved impulse voices, the positional hull-stress layer, the room tone and in-budget `hal` voices, WHEN the pool is exhausted, THEN none is stolen or ducked, and the seam-cut TOOL loop is neither ducked nor stealable by a tier >= 4 request. [AA-11, AA-12, D.18]
- **AC-AA-68 (auto)** GIVEN ambience, a TOOL loop, the seam-cut TOOL loop and the pod leg bed under a beat, WHEN D.18 runs, THEN the first three, excluding the seam-cut loop, duck by `DUCK_DB` with attack 6, hold 24 and release 30 ticks, overlapping impulses never sum, and the seam-cut loop, tier-1 to 3 cues, Horror cues and `hal` voices are not ducked; GIVEN a late tell with beat spacing 45 (under the 60-tick duck length), THEN the duck is continuous and its depth never deepens, and the D.18 envelope value at tell tick 150 matches the worked example. [AA-11, D.18]
- **AC-AA-69 (lint)** GIVEN the bus graph, WHEN linted, THEN no compressor, limiter or sidechain targets a tier-1 bus. [AA-11, L-8, M3]
- **AC-AA-70 (auto)** GIVEN any bus muted or the headless dummy driver, WHEN the simulation runs 3600 ticks, THEN `state_hash` is unchanged and every cue request is still produced. [AA-3, AUD-9, AC-AT-09]

### G. Restore, pause, death and settings

- **AC-AA-71 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Restore. Each lettered case is its own test and fails independently.
  - **AC-AA-71a** GIVEN restores into TELL, BREACH, CALM after ABORTED, a live lie and a result 10 ticks old (a fixture whose producer does not clear; Assembly's `post_restore` clears it), WHEN the first post-restore poll runs, THEN no ONESHOT request is emitted.
  - **AC-AA-71b** the bed fades in over `RESTORE_FADE_TICKS` (60): `g` = 0.383, 0.707, 0.924, 1.0 at k = 15, 30, 45, 60.
  - **AC-AA-71c** a `RESTORE_FADE_TICKS` of 0 gives `g` 1 immediately.
  - **AC-AA-71d** the lie's leak resumes at full leak level on the first frame.
  - **AC-AA-71e** `last_seen_T` is primed. [AA-5, D.4, D.12, E-AA-38 to E-AA-43]
- **AC-AA-72 (auto)** GIVEN a restore that returns an earlier epoch than `e_seen`, WHEN the next real onset (a higher epoch) is published, THEN the onset is requested (never a false calm). [AA-4, D.1, E-AA-40]
- **AC-AA-73 (auto)** GIVEN a pause during a REACH lead-in, a release and a restore fade, WHEN `T` is held, THEN each holds its position (`k`, `t_r` and `seek` are functions of sim `T`), UI requests continue, and the sim is unaffected. [AA-5, E-AA-44] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The criterion holds because no `Tween`, `Timer` or wall-clock call exists in world-voice code (lint L-15); every envelope is read back as a function of `T`.
- **AC-AA-74 (auto)** GIVEN a death stub restore and a save, WHEN processed, THEN no request is made for either and no content branches on `restore_reason`. [AA-34, `SL:23`, E-AA-45]
- **AC-AA-75 (review)** GIVEN the settings boundary rules, WHEN the design is reviewed against the data lint of AC-AA-104, THEN the World slider covers `wit`, `ann`, `run`, `attn`, `hor`, `suit`, `foot`, `ia`, `asm`, `body`, `pod`, `p1c` and `hal` as one group, the Ambience slider covers `amb` only, no setting lowers the bed below its margin, mono keeps the bed, reduce-flashing and reduce-motion never reduce audio, and no setting displays or sonifies the Attention level. [AA-43]
- **AC-AA-76 (review)** GIVEN the captions policy, WHEN reviewed against the data lint of AC-AA-104, THEN `wit`, `ann`, `run`, Suit body, foley, the three terminal interaction sounds and UI are captionable with precise source-naming, and the tell, breach, alarm cadence, pre-tell creaks, Horror and `hal` are never captioned, so no caption is a lie oracle. [AA-41, R-6]

### I. Performance and feasibility

- **AC-AA-80 (spike)** GIVEN the worst-case scene (derelict B, stage 3, a told breach with a REACH, a TOOL channel active), WHEN the audio poll, `cue_requests` and node updates are profiled for 10,000 frames on the reference machine, THEN the audio main-thread cost is at most `AUDIO_FRAME_BUDGET_US` (500 `[PLACEHOLDER]`) at the 95th percentile, and `Performance.OBJECT_COUNT` and `MEMORY_STATIC` are flat after a 300-frame warm-up (no per-frame allocation in steady state); the result decides whether `VOICE_POOL_SIZE` 48 is adopted (OQ-AA-9). The Kernel's `STEP_BUDGET_MS` does not cover Audio (a presentation subscriber, CR-11). [AA-3]
- **AC-AA-81 (spike)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* GIVEN the 640x360 world SubViewport at every integer zoom step, WHEN `AudioStreamPlayer2D` with an `AudioListener2D` on the avatar (built-in attenuation disabled) is compared with a manual pan, THEN pan is within 0.05 of the D.9 world-px model at every step, or the manual pan is adopted; gain is the manual D.9 value written per voice in both arms, so the spike does not compare gain, and the tell bed is unaffected either way. [AA-9, OQ-AA-1]

### J. Rules, formulas and edge cases the first set left open (added by the QA review)

- **AC-AA-82 (auto)** Tier per family. GIVEN one published fact per row of the AA-2 truth-class table, WHEN requests are produced, THEN each request's `family`, `tier` and `kind` equal the table row (witness, `ann`, running, footsteps and Suit tier 1; tell impulses, strike and REACH resolve tier 2; hull-stress, creep, ingress and egress creak, `p1c` and `hal` tier 3; interaction, assembly, body, creaks and ambience tier 4; music tier 5). [AA-2, AA-11]
- **AC-AA-83 (lint)** No sim footprint. GIVEN the audio source and the kernel registries, WHEN scanned and booted, THEN no step hook, state block, mutator, command or `push_stimulus` is registered by Audio, and a save made after 600 ticks contains no audio key. [AA-3]
- **AC-AA-84 (auto + lint)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Request schema and a read-only poll. GIVEN any request, THEN it carries the fields of the `cue_request` record (`cue_id`, `family`, `tier`, `kind`, `anchor`, `transmission`, `gain`/`pitch`/`rate`, `start_T`/`seek`, `listener_scope`, plus `leak_flags` for `hal`) and `cue_id` never appears under two families; and the poll is read-only: a **snapshot test** compares `state_hash` and every published view before and after a 600-tick soak with the audio module running and finds them equal, and a **source lint** finds no call to a mutator, a command or `push_stimulus` and no assignment into a published view from the audio module. (GDScript cannot disable writes at run time, so no "writes disabled" mode is asserted.) [AA-3]
- **AC-AA-85 (auto)** Sustained resume. GIVEN a restore with a live running loop, witness class, Suit loop, hull-stress layer and seam-cut loop, WHEN the first poll runs, THEN each is requested at steady level with only a `DECLICK_TICKS` (2) fade and no attack-transient variant. [AA-5]
- **AC-AA-86 (lint)** Bus and tag matrix. GIVEN the identity table, THEN every family maps to exactly one bus, the pairwise intersection of sound ids, source recordings and timbre tags is empty for every pair among `attn`, `wit`, `ann`, `hor`, `p1c` and `hal`, and the Voice bus carries only `vox`. [AA-6 h, AA-39]
- **AC-AA-87 (lint)** Slice completeness. GIVEN the identity table, THEN each P0 and P1 cue named in AA-7 (onset, beat, bed and three tier layers, hull-stress, release, breach entry, alarm pulse, subside, pre-tell creak; the eight witness classes; four running timbres plus the INTEGRITY tone; footsteps walk, run and carry; the reading-lie signature under both Filter ids; Horror's four kinds) has a row with an asset or a registered placeholder, no `mus` asset exists, and with the home-ship running at least one sustained stream is active. [AA-7, AA-40]
- **AC-AA-88 (lint)** Verbatim names. GIVEN the event inventory of AA-8, THEN every consumed key and event name matches the owner's published name exactly and an unknown name fails boot. [AA-8]
- **AC-AA-89 (auto)** Structure-borne set. GIVEN the hull-stress layer, the seam-cut loop and Horror's ingress and egress creak behind a shut door and one hull boundary, THEN each has `A_t` = 15 x 0.25 x 1 = 3.75 dB and no door term; and a lint finds no `Area2D` `audio_bus_override`. [AA-10, D.9]
- **AC-AA-90 (auto)** Creak at onset. GIVEN a creak started 10 ticks before ONSET, THEN it plays to its end, no new creak starts from the ONSET poll on, none starts during BREACH or SUBSIDING, and the first resumes only after CALM at step >= 1. [AA-14, AA-22, E-AA-6]
- **AC-AA-91 (auto)** Beat variants. GIVEN 21 consecutive beats of one epoch and of two epochs, THEN no two consecutive variant indices are equal, the index is a pure function of `(tell_epoch, tell_beat)`, and `gain` is identical for every beat index. [AA-15]
- **AC-AA-92 (auto)** Bed scope *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* (the bed begins its `BED_END_TICKS` fade at BREACH entry rather than stopping). GIVEN a tell with the listener in the home-ship, a derelict, a pod in LEG and a `NOT_LIVE` home-ship, at zoom steps 1 to k_auto+2, THEN the bed request has `anchor` NONE, `transmission` NONE, `listener_scope` ALL_LIVING and identical `gain` in all cases; the bed is requested only while `phase == TELL` and begins its `BED_END_TICKS` fade at BREACH entry; a mono downmix fixture preserves non-zero energy in both bed bands. [AA-16, E-AA-55]
- **AC-AA-93 (auto)** Hull anchor. GIVEN a segment with plates at (96, 144) and (144, 144) and `section_offset` (480, -96), THEN `hull_anchor` is (600, 48); with an odd sum the integer division floors; the lowest-id segment is used. The Layout read is a stub `(provisional — Layout hull_anchor, CX-A4)`. [D.16]
- **AC-AA-94 (auto)** Breach exemption. GIVEN BREACH on a derelict with no `alarm_eligible` descriptor and no beacon, THEN the entry event and the pulse cadence are requested, and the bed request stops at the BREACH_ENTER poll. [AA-20 e, g]
- **AC-AA-95 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Alarm count and `beacon_live`. GIVEN `BREACH_MIN_TICKS` and `BREACH_MAX_TICKS`, THEN in FALLBACK the pulse counts, entry event included, are 8 for a 900-tick and 29 for a 3600-tick BREACH (sweep 128); in ALIGNED with the first hit at 191 ticks they are 7 and 28 (at 64 ticks, 8 and 29); the debounced `beacon_live_f` is true only when the raw read has held for `BEACON_LIVE_HOLD_TICKS` ticks, the raw read needs all of kind BEACON, home-ship body and `output_pm` > 0, and the listener is on that body (each conjunct toggled once; the Emitter read is a stub `(provisional — Emitter read, CX-A15)`); the first mode choice is made at `breach_enter_T` + 14; a `breach_enter_T` of 19968 (a grid hit) gives one `attn` pulse, not two, because pulse 0 is the one `attn` start permitted on that tick by identity (the grid hit is not a second pulse and is not suppressed by a gap against itself). [D.2, E-AA-11]
- **AC-AA-96 (auto)** No phantom tell. GIVEN published CALM with `level_pm` swept 0 to 1000 and sanity stages 0 to 3, THEN no onset, beat, bed, release, breach or alarm request is produced, only pre-tell creaks at the quantised step. [AA-23, AA-38]
- **AC-AA-97 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Witness class drivers. Each lettered case is its own test.
  - **AC-AA-97a** DRIP 0.5 Hz at `iota` 1 and 4.0 Hz at 1000; TICK 0.5 and 3.0 Hz; SEAL_STRAIN CREAK 0.2 and 1.0 Hz (the rates are mean spacings, AC-AA-144).
  - **AC-AA-97b** HUM_DROP 0 and -600 cents at `iota` 0 and 1000.
  - **AC-AA-97c** CLANK: exactly one request at `since_steps` 0 and none afterward.
  - **AC-AA-97d** WIND: pan from `dir` 0 to 7 and no pan at 8.
  - **AC-AA-97e** SIZZLE: level by `L_w`.
  - **AC-AA-97f** every class radius is at most 609 px (`R_AUDIBLE_MAX_PX`, D.8). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
  - **AC-AA-97g** the audio anchor equals the trail anchor (same Layout composition), and the class stops with no completion cue.
  - **AC-AA-97h** an alarm-grid snap is never applied to a witness class, so a descriptor first seen at an off-grid tick starts its class on that poll. [AA-24, AA-25, D.8, FT AC-137]
- **AC-AA-98 (auto)** Running mapping endpoints. GIVEN `served_pm` 1, 500 and 1000, THEN level is -41.99, -39.0 and -36.0 dBr (+/- 0.01) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* and pitch is -299.7, -150 and 0 cents (a node at 0 is not running, so -300 is never requested); DESTROYED, SALVAGED and ABSENT are silent like FAILED; DEGRADED plays at its reduced `served_pm`; four loops at the ceiling sum to -29.98 dBr (about -30). [D.7, AA-26]
- **AC-AA-99 (auto)** Annunciator clock. GIVEN a descriptor that becomes `alarm_eligible` at `T` mod 128 = 40 outside BREACH, THEN its witness class is requested on that poll, the first `ann` pulse starts at the next `T mod 128 == 0`, and a descriptor dropping eligibility mid-pulse lets the pulse finish (at most `W`) and starts no more. [AA-27, E-AA-16, E-AA-17]
- **AC-AA-100 (auto)** Horror positional cues. GIVEN `horror.view` in LURK, STALK, RETREAT (moving), TRANSIT, LURK stationary, and an ingress and an egress event, THEN the creep request exists and is positional at `pos_px` only while moving in LURK, STALK or RETREAT (none in TRANSIT, which has no `HE:1193` creep row), the ingress and egress creak is structure-borne at the `segment_at` anchor, and with `pos_px` and Horror state held fixed `gain` is a function of physical falloff only (no proximity or state-keyed cue). [AA-28 a, d, AA-29]
- **AC-AA-101 (auto)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Suit cues, for each named cue state (breath steps normal, closed-reserve, thin-air and load; valve click engage and disengage; seam hiss; cell whine at `CELL_WARN_S` (20); rattle; fan; gasp). Each lettered case is its own test.
  - **AC-AA-101a** the request exists if and only if the Suit's true state does.
  - **AC-AA-101b** it is produced no later than the visual notification (the frame poll precedes the draw).
  - **AC-AA-101c** it is identical at sanity stages 0 to 3.
  - **AC-AA-101d** it cannot be suppressed or added by a Filter lie.
  - **AC-AA-101e** the Suit emits no noise to Attention; the Chemlight glow has no request, its throw, land and wane do. [AA-30, AA-38]
- **AC-AA-102 (manual)** Breath blind test. GIVEN load-breath and thin-air-breath clips, THEN pooled identification is at least 90 per cent (panel protocol); the capture and trial log are retained (the audible half of AC-SE-42). [AA-30, AC-SE-42] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The three Suit body cues ruled into MVP (breath cadence, valve click, visor-fog step; D92) are in the MVP gate with real assets for the kill/continue playtest; the other Suit cues stay excluded from the gate while they are placeholders, with closing evidence at the Vertical Slice. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-AA-103 (auto)** Footfall states. GIVEN a slowed zone at half speed (120 ticks of walking), stagger, work, idle, down, and a diagonal walk of 120 ticks, THEN footfalls are 3 in 120 ticks while slowed (one per 40), 0 in each time-stepped state, and 6 on the diagonal. [AA-31, D.14, E-AA-37]
- **AC-AA-104 (lint)** Policy as data. GIVEN the identity table, THEN `captionable` is true only for `wit`, `ann`, `run`, `suit`, `foot`, `ia` terminals, `asm`, `body`, `pod` and `ui`, and false for `attn`, `hor`, `hal`, `p1c`, `amb` and creaks; the World slider group lists exactly the 13 tokens of AA-43 and the Ambience group contains only `amb`. [AA-41, AA-43]
- **AC-AA-105 (auto)** Interaction cues. GIVEN `transition_seq` and `result_seq` sequences for cancel, abort, nothing-happened and done, THEN only the terminal transition's cue is requested, the four sequences differ pairwise, the TAP sound is requested at the Applied result, a deferred rejection requests nothing until its reveal, and a restore requests no abort cue (the audio half of AC-IA-100). [AA-32]
- **AC-AA-106 (auto)** Outcome gate. GIVEN `last_result.T` 1000 and `OUTCOME_CUE_WINDOW_TICKS` (60), THEN the poll at 1010 plays, the poll at 1011 plays nothing, a first poll at 1100 plays nothing, and a restore at 5000 with `last_result.T` 4990 (a fixture whose producer does not clear) plays nothing; the weld bead settle is the single "done" request for a weld and the first weld has mix room. [D.12, AA-33]
- **AC-AA-107 (auto)** No value, no reward. GIVEN a carried item of any value or condition, a kill-class event, a sanity stage boundary, a debunk, a recovery, a save and a death, THEN no request is produced by any of them. [AA-34]
- **AC-AA-108 (lint)** Signature assets. GIVEN every `hal` asset, THEN a fringe partial exists in the reserved band (`HAL_FRINGE_BAND_HZ`, about 1200 to 1800 Hz), the breathing period is within `HAL_BREATH_PERIOD_TICKS` 240 to 360 ticks (4 to 6 s), the tail exceeds the source by the asset-spec value, the peak is at least `HAL_PEAK_BELOW_BEAT_DB` (3) under the loudest `attn` beat, and no other family has energy in the reserved band; the breathing envelope's peak normalised cross-correlation against the `tell_beat` schedule and against the alarm sweep is below `BREATH_LOCK_CEIL` (Filter, AC-PF-27). [AA-35, AA-37, D.15]
- **AC-AA-109 (auto, provisional PF-20)** Lie privacy and falsifier. GIVEN seat A lying and seat B asking, THEN B receives no `hal` request for A's lie; `hal` requests are `OWNER_PRIVATE`; a world-surface reference is positional and a card or HUD reference is non-positional; and a lying gauge over a stalling pump still produces the pump's witness or running request unchanged (the automated falsifier). [AA-36, AA-37 e, E-AA-49, E-AA-52]
- **AC-AA-110 (auto, integration)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Audio first, all cues. GIVEN a soak of one onset, 20 beats, the tier steps, an abort, a breach, a subside and one of each tell-class fact (a loop failure, a descriptor, a Suit state, a strike), WHEN the tick-stamped capture rig records the audio-request frame and the first visual-draw frame of every event, THEN `request_frame <= first_visual_draw_frame` for every event, with the request created by the frame poll that precedes the presenter's draw of that frame. [HR-9, AA-13, AAV sync direction rule]
- **AC-AA-111 (lint)** Asset spec. GIVEN the asset directory, THEN every loop, bed and sound longer than `PCM_MAX_TICKS` (60) is OGG Vorbis and every shorter one-shot is 16-bit PCM *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*, all at 48 kHz (mono for positional sources, stereo only for bed and ambience), every stream is preloaded at boot, the project mix rate is pinned to 48000 `[UNV]`, the `wit` asset count is at most `WIT_MVP_ASSET_CAP` (16), carries momentary-loudness metadata, has a beat tail at most `BEAT_TAIL_MAX_TICKS` (60), a pre-tell tail at most `PRETELL_TAIL_MAX_TICKS`, the total size is under `AUDIO_ASSET_BUDGET_MB` (150 `[PLACEHOLDER]`, valid once OQ-AA-12 confirms it), and no audio value (level, radius, interval, class table) is hard-coded in source. [AA-44, D.10]
- **AC-AA-112 (lint)** Tactile channel boundaries (AA-42). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN the source, THEN no call to `Input.start_joy_vibration`, `Input.vibrate_handheld` or an equivalent is reachable from any `attn`, `hor` or bed code path except through the single beat-pulse module, which (a) is reachable only when `haptic_beat_pulse` is true (default false), (b) is called only from the BEAT edge of D.1, with the fixed `HAPTIC_BEAT_MS` and `HAPTIC_BEAT_STRENGTH_PM` and no argument derived from `tier`, `tell_elapsed`, the beat index or `level_pm`; and the Suit body-cue haptic path is keyed to Suit state only (D44). [AA-42, L-18]
- **AC-AA-113 (auto)** Context invariance. GIVEN a fixed published state, THEN the requests for bed, beats, witness classes and `hal` are identical across listener contexts (home-ship, derelict, LEG, `NOT_LIVE`) except for the alarm clock reference, door penalties and the pod leg bed; the pod leg bed envelope never modifies the bed. [AA-46, E-AA-54, E-AA-55]
- **AC-AA-114 (auto)** Transmission edges. GIVEN `dx` 144 px and `PAN_SPAN_TILES` 6 (288 px), pan is 0.5; `dx` 400 gives 1.0; `dx` -144 gives -0.5; a source reachable by two paths takes the lower attenuation; a half-open door (4.5 dB) plus one hull boundary (15 dB) caps at 18 dB giving -38.94 dBr for the -13.80 dBr HISS at 6 tiles (`A_d` 7.13 dB under the log law; -43.80 under the superseded linear law) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*; a non-witness cue below `AUDIBLE_CULL_DB` (-56) is not started. [D.9]
- **AC-AA-115 (auto)** Allocator determinism and reserve. GIVEN the same request set in 20 shuffled orders, THEN the chosen victim and dropped set are identical (key: tier, quietest, oldest, `cue_id` lexicographic); a tier-4 request is dropped when only the 2 reserved impulse voices are free; reserved voices are never lent to another family. [D.10, AA-12]
- **AC-AA-116 (auto, VS-B dormant)** Co-op hooks. GIVEN a dead seat and two seats with `tell_on`, THEN no bed request is addressed to the dead seat, two P1 cues play with different hash phases, and neither plays on its own source's client. [AA-46, E-AA-64 to E-AA-66]
- **AC-AA-117 (auto)** Flow-zero TICK at k = 1. GIVEN a flow-zero hull pre-stage TICK descriptor at zoom k = 1 and Filter stages 0 to 3, THEN the TICK request is produced, is identical at every stage, and is the only audio request that descriptor yields; the descriptor carries `sev` 1 to 2 and `intensity_pm` 0 (mode-5 STRUCT pre-stage), and the request's rate comes from `sev` and `since_steps`, not `iota` (D.8) (discharges FT AC-144). [AA-24, AA-38, D.8, L-13]
- **AC-AA-125 (auto)** Dedupe edge cases. GIVEN a hitch spanning an abort, `TELL_REARM_TICKS` and a new onset, and a BREACH to CALM change with SUBSIDING skipped, WHEN the next poll runs, THEN the first plays ONSET only (no RELEASE) and the second plays nothing and stops the alarm cadence. [E-AA-67, E-AA-68, D.1]

### K. Visual/Audio sync and policy (from the art-director proposal)

- **AC-AA-118 (lint)** No audio-driven visual. GIVEN the presenter and shader source, WHEN scanned, THEN no presenter symbol reads Audio, and no visual parameter (position, scale, brightness, hue, alpha, shake) references an audio level, bus meter, cue start or voice state; no screen flash, vignette, camera nudge or HUD pulse is produced on a tell beat, strike or breach pulse. [AAV-M1, AT V14, AV-4]
- **AC-AA-119 (manual)** Dark room, no marker. GIVEN a real HISS in a compartment with zero lit emitters, WHEN the capture is made with screen recording, THEN the audio is present at its true level and **no** visual marker of any kind (ripple, ping, outline, glow, radar) appears; the capture is retained. [AAV-14, Principle 1]
- **AC-AA-120 (manual)** Bed with nothing lit. GIVEN nothing lit and a TELL active, WHEN the listener is on a derelict, in a pod and in the home-ship, THEN the bed is present for the listener in each case; the three captures are retained. [AAV-6, AAV-12]
- **AC-AA-121 (manual)** Accessibility invariance. GIVEN the same breach captured with default settings, with reduce-flashing on and with reduce-motion on, WHEN the three audio captures are compared by waveform difference, THEN they are identical within the capture noise floor. [AAV-9, M-4, M1]
- **AC-AA-122 (auto)** Foot-contact flag. GIVEN the character sidecar export, THEN frame 0 and frame 4 of every `walk_<facing>`, `run_<facing>` and `carry_<facing>` cycle carry the `foot` flag and `FOOT_CONTACT_FRAMES` equals the exported set (it is exported, not typed in code). [AAV-28, M-6, D.14]
- **AC-AA-123 (lint, Alpha)** Caption and settings plates. [AAV-38, AA-41, AA-43]
- **AC-AA-124 (manual)** Silent boundaries. GIVEN a sanity stage boundary, a debunk, a recovery, a death and a save, WHEN captured with screen and audio, THEN no audio cue, visual flash, vignette or HUD glyph is produced by Audio at any of them; the capture is retained. [AAV-36, AAV-37, AA-34, AA-38]
- **AC-AA-127 (auto)** No-`iota` witnesses are never silent. GIVEN a SHORT descriptor (mode 2) at `sev` 2 with `intensity_pm` 0 and `medium != NONE`, WHEN requests are produced, THEN a SIZZLE request exists at `L_w(2)` = -21 + 12 * (2 - 1) / 3 = **-17.0 dBr** at zero distance *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*; GIVEN the same descriptor at `sev` 1 and 4, THEN -21.0 and -9.0 dBr; GIVEN each of the nine FT-30 ids (SHORT, OVERLOAD, OVERHEAT, SCRAM, FREEZE, ATMO_COLD, ATMO_HOT, ATMO_THIN, ATMO_DECOMP) and the mode-5 STRUCT pre-stage at `sev` >= 1, THEN none yields zero requests while `medium != NONE`, and lint L-13 finds no request path that silences one; GIVEN a SEAL_STRAIN with medium HEAT, acoustic TICK, `sev` 1 and `intensity_pm` 0, THEN a TICK request exists at `L_w(1)` = -21.0 dBr; a LEAK at `iota` 0 stays silent. *(provisional — Graph adoption of FT-30 item c)* [AA-24, AA-25, D.8, E-AA-19, L-13]
- **AC-AA-128 (manual, Visual/Audio)** The berth is witnessed, not asserted (discharges AC-WM-117). GIVEN a berth in a launched build, played from the `berth` press to the weld prompt, with the audio captured and retained in `production/qa/evidence/`, THEN a sustained non-positional drive-hum and hull-strain bed is audible from the edge into BERTHING to the berth contact cue (it fades in and out over `LEG_BED_FADE_TICKS` and stops at `BERTHED`), it is not keyed to any progress datum (a test double of the audio source finds no read of `t0`, `n` or any elapsed or remaining tick), five testers who have not seen the asset list do not mistake it for the Tell when it is played against a recorded Tell, the contact cue and the weld prompt follow it, and under reduce-motion the bed, the contact cue and the prompt remain; GIVEN a TELL active during a BERTHING, THEN the berth bed ducks under each beat and the beat count is unchanged. - **AC-AA-129 (auto, provisional PF-20)** `hal` audible radius (added at CD-GDD-ALIGN; discharges the P4 verification economy). GIVEN a lying world-surface gauge over a machine with a running or witness sound, `HAL_RADIUS_PX` 192, `legibility_range_px(ref)` of 288 and then of 144, and a listener at 1, 3, 4, 5 and 8 tiles, WHEN requests and voices are computed, THEN `R_hal` is 192 in the first case and 144 in the second; outside `R_hal` no `hal` voice exists (the leak is inaudible) while the machine's true running or witness request is unchanged and still audible at its ordinary level and audible floor; inside `R_hal` the leak sounds at its D.15 level; the boundary crossing fades over `HAL_FADE_TICKS` with no sting; a card or HUD reading is non-positional and is not radius-gated; and a data file that sets `HAL_RADIUS_PX` above `legibility_range_px(ref)` of any surface is clamped to it with a DIAGNOSTIC. - **AC-AA-130 (auto)** Instrument stress always has a sound (added at the 2026-10-27 consistency pass; discharges AC-IW-106 and OQ-IW-32 for Audio). - **AC-AA-131 (auto)** Alarm boundary offsets at entry (added by the design review). - **AC-AA-133 (auto)** Home-ship mode choice and a toggling beacon (added by the design review). - **AC-AA-132 (auto, integration)** Catch-up frame (added by the design review). - **AC-AA-136 (auto)** Caps, drop order and hysteresis (added by the design review). GIVEN eight witness voices playing and a ninth requested at `sev` 3, THEN the lowest-`sev`, quietest tier-1 voice that is quieter than the requester is replaced and `voice_tier1_steal` increments once; GIVEN a ninth quieter than all eight, THEN it is dropped and `voice_drop_tier1` increments; GIVEN a DRIP at 4.0 Hz, THEN `V_wit` is 2; GIVEN four running loops and a fifth 2 dB above the weakest for 30 ticks, THEN the set is unchanged, and GIVEN 3.5 dB for 30 ticks, THEN the weakest is replaced, both fade over 12 ticks and `run_cap_drop` increments once; GIVEN a loop within `NEAR_PX`, THEN it enters at once; GIVEN a listener moving from -2 to +2 tiles across the point equidistant between two machines in 1 px steps with the set full, THEN membership changes at most once. - **AC-AA-138 (manual)** The anchor moment reproduced (added by the design review; the pass that confirms the user ruling of OQ-AA-32 (b)). GIVEN a derelict with a running COOLANT pump two rooms aft (one closed door between) and a seam weep ahead and to the left in the next compartment, the lamp off, WHEN each tester stands in the compartment, THEN at least 4 of 5 testers name the coolant pump by timbre and say on which side the weep is, and the mist is where the ear said; the capture and trial log are retained. *Prediction at the placeholders (D.9, D.19):* the weep clause passes (a seam weep at `iota` 80 is -20.05 dBr at the source and -27.18 dBr at 288 px, about 12 dB over the room tone); the pump clause is **expected to fail** at the placeholders, because the loop is only floor-protected (-54 dBr) and the room tone masks it beyond about 250 px (5.2 tiles) in the open (R-g, 11.77 dB short); the pass then moves `RUN_CEIL_DB` (within D.6), `INTEGRITY_TONE_DB`, `R_RUN_AUDIBLE_PX` or `DIST_SLOPE_DB` until the pump clause passes, and records the values (OQ-AA-44). [Player Fantasy anchor, D.9, D.19 R-g] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-AA-139 (auto)** Duck breakpoints (added by the design review). GIVEN beats at spacings 150, 60, 45 and 30 ticks, THEN the victim gain follows the D.18 breakpoints: at 150 it is back at 0 dB before the next beat; at 60 it reaches 0 dB exactly at the next beat; at 45 it is -3.00 dB at the next beat and re-attacks to -6; at 30 it stays at -6; it never goes below -6; an `ia_done` voice is ducked while a tier-1 footfall on the same Foley bus is not. - **AC-AA-140 (auto)** Transmission clamps and hull holes (added by the design review). GIVEN `area / area_max` of 1.4, 1.0, 0.4, 0 and an unknown door, THEN `open_pm` is 1000, 1000, 400, 0 and 1000 (never above 1000) and the door terms are 0, 0, 5.4, 9 and 0 dB; GIVEN one hull boundary with no hole, a hole at `open_pm` 600 and a fully open hole, THEN the hull terms are 15, 6 and 0 dB; `A_t` is never negative and never above 18. [D.9, E-AA-23] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-AA-141 (auto)** Pod leg bed edges (added by the design review). GIVEN legs of `n` = 1, 11, 12 and 90, THEN `n` = 1 and 11 make no request, `n` = 12 gives a triangle peaking at 1.0 at `t0` + 6, and `n` = 90 a triangle peaking at `t0` + 45. [D.17] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-AA-142 (auto)** REACH lead-in hold (added by the design review). GIVEN `reach_end_T` 7140 and `strike_T` 7140, THEN the lead-in's end and the strike are contiguous; GIVEN an off-grid fixture with `strike_T` 7144, THEN the lead-in holds 4 ticks and the strike starts at 7144; GIVEN no `strike_T` seen within 4 ticks, THEN the lead-in decays over `DECLICK_TICKS`; a fizzle is AC-AA-43. [D.11] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-AA-143 (manual)** Five-minute annunciator (added by the design review; promoted to a pass criterion by the user ruling of OQ-AA-33 (b)). GIVEN a descriptor that stays eligible for 5 minutes on the tester's body, and a second descriptor of equal `sev` that becomes eligible at minute 4, WHEN each tester plays on and is asked afterwards (i) whether the alarm still told them anything in the last minute and (ii) whether they noticed a second descriptor becoming eligible, THEN at least 4 of 5 testers rate the stepped-down klaxon 2 or less on a 5-point "fatiguing" scale and at least 4 of 5 notice the second descriptor without a prompt (the restore to full level); the capture, the level trace (0 dB for sweeps 0 to 7, -6 dB from sweep 8, 0 dB again at the new descriptor) and the answers are retained. [AA-27, D.20] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **AC-AA-144 (auto)** Rate-class recurrence (added by the design review). GIVEN a DRIP at 0.5, 2.0 and 4.0 Hz over 360,000 ticks with a fixed seed, THEN the mean spacing is within 5 per cent of `M_c` (120, 30, 15), no two events are closer than `G_c` (60, 15, 7), a rate change takes effect from the next scanned tick, and a restore scans nothing retroactively. [D.8] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-AA-145 (manual)** The negative gate (added by the design review). GIVEN a panel that has played CALM with the creak ladder stepping through its four steps, a monster present and sanity stages 0 to 3, WHEN each tester is asked to state the Attention level, the distance of the monster and their own sanity stage by sound alone, THEN no tester can state the Attention level more precisely than one ladder step, none can judge the monster's distance from any sound other than its own positional creep, and none can state their sanity stage; the result sheet is retained. [Player Fantasy, AA-22, AA-28, AA-38] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-AA-146 (auto)** D.9 log-distance law (user ruling OQ-AA-32 (b)). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN `NEAR_PX` 96 and `DIST_SLOPE_DB` 4.5, WHEN `A_d` is evaluated at `d` 0, 96, 192, 288, 384, 576 and 609 px, THEN it is 0, 0, 4.50, 7.13, 9.00, 11.63 and 12.00 dB (+/- 0.01), is non-decreasing in `d`, and is identical at every zoom step and camera lead; GIVEN the defaults, THEN `R_AUDIBLE_MAX_PX` = floor(96 * 2^(12 / 4.5)) = 609 and every default class radius (576, 576, 576, 576, 480, 384, 288, 288) is at most 609; GIVEN `DIST_SLOPE_DB` 6, THEN `R_AUDIBLE_MAX_PX` is 384 and the 576 rows are refused at load. [D.8, D.9]
- **AC-AA-147 (auto)** Running-loop audibility floor (D.9). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN a COOLANT loop at `served_pm` 1000 (-36 dBr) 576 px away behind one closed door, THEN `L` before the floor is -56.63 dBr and the loop is requested at `RUN_AUDIBLE_FLOOR_DB` (-54); GIVEN the same loop at 700 px behind the door, THEN `L` is -57.90 and no request is made (the cull applies outside `R_RUN_AUDIBLE_PX`); GIVEN five audible loops, THEN the `RUN_MAX_VOICES` set is ranked on the unclamped `L_listener`, so a floor-lifted far loop never displaces a nearer one; GIVEN a FAILED node, THEN the floor does not apply. [D.7, D.9, E-AA-77]
- **AC-AA-148 (auto)** Annunciator step-down (D.20; user ruling OQ-AA-33 (b)). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN a descriptor that becomes `alarm_eligible` at `T` 40 on the listener's body and stays eligible, WHEN the `ann` pulses at `T` 128 to 1024 and at 1152 are requested, THEN the pulses through 1024 carry trim 0 dB (`sweeps_held` 7 at 1024) and the pulse at 1152 carries -6 dB (`sweeps_held` 8); GIVEN a second eligible descriptor at `T` 1500 (or a rise in `sev`), THEN the pulse at 1536 carries 0 dB; GIVEN a restore at `T` 3000 into a stepped-down state, THEN `T_e` is 3000, the pulses at 3072 to 3968 carry 0 dB and the pulse at 4096 carries -6 dB; the rhythm (one pulse per `BEACON_SWEEP_TICKS`) is identical in every case and the trim is constant within a pulse. [AA-27, D.20, E-AA-73, E-AA-74]
- **AC-AA-149 (auto)** Haptic beat pulse (AA-42 exception (ii); user ruling D51). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN `haptic_beat_pulse` false (the default), WHEN a full tell runs, THEN no vibration call is made; GIVEN it true and a published tell with beats 0 to 5, THEN exactly one pulse per BEAT edge (six, beat 0 being the onset), each with the same `HAPTIC_BEAT_MS` and `HAPTIC_BEAT_STRENGTH_PM`, and none for a tier step, the release, the breach entry, an alarm pulse, a pre-tell creak or a Horror cue; GIVEN a 3-second hitch publishing beats 2 and 3, THEN one pulse (for beat 3); GIVEN a restore into TELL, THEN none until the next published beat. [AA-42, AA-15, D.1, E-AA-76]
- **AC-AA-150 (auto)** Frozen escalation (X4; OQ-AA-34 resolved). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN an `attention.view` fixture in which `tier` stays 1 from tell tick 0 to the abort at tick 900 (the escalation frozen), WHEN the poll runs at 1-tick intervals, THEN no tier-2 or tier-3 layer and no hull-stress request is made, the beats are exactly those published, the release starts from the tier-1 bed level (`L_bed0` = `BED_BASE_DB`) and `RELEASE_TICKS` 90 holds; GIVEN a fixture whose `tier` rises 1, 2, 3 on the escalation clock, THEN the layers arrive as in AC-AA-7; GIVEN a published `tier` that drops inside a TELL, THEN it is treated as a restore re-prime and no cue is made (E-AA-75). [AA-18, AA-19, D.3]
- **AC-AA-151 (manual)** Body cues, muted and sound arms (D92; HS-DT-1). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* GIVEN the kill/continue playtest build with real assets for breath cadence, the reserve valve click and the visor-fog step, WHEN a muted arm and a sound arm each play the same scripted reserve-engage and thin-air sequence, THEN in the sound arm each of the three cues starts no later than its visual and at least 4 of 5 testers identify the reserve engaging from sound alone with the HUD hidden, and in the muted arm the visual and HUD cues alone carry the same state (no information exists only in the audio); the captures and result sheets of both arms are retained. [AA-30, AA-7]

### Gaps / untestable as written

- **The bed margin (AC-AA-8a, AC-AA-8b, AC-AA-14) is unprovable headlessly.** The data-load check (AC-AA-9, AC-AA-13) proves the arithmetic; whether the bed is audible on laptop speakers is a listening test on a retained capture. No automated test closes it. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **The reserved-band lint (AC-AA-78, AC-AA-108) has unproven feasibility.** The spectral-centroid and band check on imported assets may be too noisy to gate on; the tag-only lint plus the blind tests is the MVP default and the spectral lint a Vertical Slice upgrade (OQ-AA-5, CD-GDD-ALIGN). The band half of AC-AA-78 is advisory until the spike lands.
- **Audible latency is a measurement, not a rule.** "Audio no later than visual" is proved as an ordering guarantee (AC-AA-5, AC-AA-110); the device-buffer and Windows-stack delay is a spike (AC-AA-6).
- **The deaf and hard-of-hearing tell channel is the visual rim plus the opt-in haptic beat pulse (AA-42).** No criterion can make a tell audible to a player who cannot hear; the ruled substitute is the beat pulse (AC-AA-149, AC-AA-112), and the criteria assert that no other setting adds one (OQ-AA-15 resolved). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)*
- **Spikes AC-AA-6, AC-AA-80 and AC-AA-81 close on a recorded result.** AC-AA-80 and AC-AA-81 carry thresholds; AC-AA-6 does not and cannot until the first measurement exists.
- **Panel feasibility.** Every `(manual)` criterion needs the panel protocol; if a 5-tester panel cannot be had, the criterion is closed as "indicative" only (OQ-AA-13).
- **Criteria on provisional reads** (AC-AA-33, AC-AA-93, AC-AA-95 against stubs; AC-AA-55, AC-AA-57, AC-AA-60, AC-AA-61, AC-AA-62, AC-AA-109 against the Filter transport) are attributed to the neighbour when they fail and re-run at the X-6 confirming scan.
- **Pod and body assets (AC-AA-53)** are wired at MVP and authored at Vertical Slice; the criterion passes with a placeholder or no asset, which proves the wiring only.

## Open Questions

*Nothing decided in this document is reopened here. Rows are the routed asks, the placeholders awaiting a measurement, the refreshes owed to neighbours, and the provisional neighbours. Owner: **user** = needs the user's decision, **agent** = a named specialist or neighbouring GDD owner, **GDD** = this document resolves it at a later pass. Target: playtest, next GDD, ADR, or a gate. The user-decision items come first; each carries the proposed default this GDD already follows. Ids OQ-AA-7, OQ-AA-8 and OQ-AA-10 are **closed** (the 2026-10-26 checkpoint rulings) and are kept so the citations in the body still resolve.* *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The three USER DECISIONS of the design review, OQ-AA-32 to OQ-AA-34, sit after OQ-AA-31 in id order and are the first to be answered; OQ-AA-35 to OQ-AA-42 are agent-owned routing and measurement rows. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* **The three USER DECISIONS and OQ-AA-2 and OQ-AA-15 were answered by the 2026-10-27 user rulings (kept with their resolutions so the citations resolve); OQ-AA-43 and OQ-AA-44 are the residual and the confirmation rows.**

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-AA-15 | **The deaf and hard-of-hearing tell channel (risk CD-5).** Audio is the only non-visual Attention channel and no indicator of any kind may exist, so a player who cannot hear has the visual rim alone (and a player who cannot see has the audio alone). Is that gap acceptable at MVP, or does the user want a deliberate exception (a caption for the tell, a visual cue, a tactile cue), accepting that each is an Attention indicator in effect and contradicts `ART:954` item 15? Proposed default followed: **no caption, no indicator, no rumble**; each channel is sufficient alone for its sense (AUD-5), and the gap is recorded as the accepted cost **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions):** the deaf or muted player in the dark gets an opt-in, off-by-default haptic pulse on tell beats (same information as the audio beat, so not an Attention indicator, D51); no caption and no visual cue are added; the previous default (no caption, no indicator, no rumble) is superseded; AA-42, AA-41. `design/accessibility-requirements.md` is to be authored later with `/ux-design accessibility`. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (with the accessibility-specialist) | `design/accessibility-requirements.md`; Accessibility Settings GDD |

| OQ-AA-32 | **RESOLVED (X12 / D86): hearing range against the fantasy** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. The Player Fantasy promises the machinery "two rooms away"; D.9's linear 3 dB per tile law with the 288 px class radius delivers about one screen, a running loop is culled about three tiles behind a closed door, and panning is horizontal only (the y position of a leak is barely conveyed). Options: **(a)** rescope the fantasy text to "in the room and through the next door"; **(b)** change D.9 to a log-distance law, re-derive `R_AUDIBLE_MAX_PX` and the floors relation, and give running loops an audibility floor, with a listening pass before it is locked (the review's recommendation, because the fantasy is the product); **(c)** in either case, decide whether a cue for the vertical axis is added (until then localisation is pan plus level, and AC-AA-36 binds the east and west sides by pan and the north and south sides by level only, because a north-south call from horizontal pan fails by construction). Previous default, superseded by the ruling: nothing changed (a: held as written, D.9 unchanged); AC-AA-138 reproduces the anchor moment and is expected to fail until the user chooses **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions):** option (b). D.9 is a log-distance law, `A_d = DIST_SLOPE_DB * log2(max(d, NEAR_PX) / NEAR_PX)` with `DIST_SLOPE_DB` 4.5 dB per doubling; `R_AUDIBLE_MAX_PX` is re-derived from the D.8 floors relation as 609 px (was 288; derivation in D.9); running loops get an audibility floor (`RUN_AUDIBLE_FLOOR_DB` -54 inside `R_RUN_AUDIBLE_PX` 576); the fantasy text stands and is not rescoped. To be confirmed after a listening pass (OQ-AA-44, AC-AA-138). Option (c), a vertical-axis cue, was not part of the ruling and stays open as OQ-AA-43. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (with the audio-director and creative-director) | before the listening pass of OQ-AA-35 |
| OQ-AA-33 | **RESOLVED (X12 / D87): the annunciator under sustained eligibility** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. For as long as a `sev >= ALARM_SEV_MIN` descriptor stays eligible the `ann` klaxon sounds every 2.1 s at tier 1, never ducked, which is the Barotrauma pattern this GDD names as an anti-reference and trains players to ignore the one alarm that reduce-motion players rely on. The listener scope (the descriptor's body only) is applied (AA-27). Options for sustained eligibility: **(a)** keep the rhythm and level unchanged (as authored); **(b)** keep the rhythm, step the level down by a fixed amount after a number of sweeps (for example 6 dB after 8 sweeps) and restore it on a new descriptor or a rise in `sev` (the review's example); **(c)** another policy. Previous default, superseded by the ruling: (a); AC-AA-143 is the five-minute listening check **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions):** option (b): keep the rhythm, step the level down by `ANN_STEPDOWN_DB` (6 dB) after `ANN_STEPDOWN_SWEEPS` (8) sweeps, restore on a new descriptor or a rise in `sev` (D.20, AA-27); AC-AA-143 is now a pass criterion and AC-AA-148 the automated check. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (with the game-designer and accessibility-specialist) | the listening pass; Accessibility Settings GDD |
| OQ-AA-34 | **RESOLVED (X4 / D88 / D48): tell escalation against the release (P2 against P3, with Attention)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. Escalation follows `tell_elapsed`, not the player's behaviour; the best realistic abort comes about 10 s after the first knock, so a player who goes still hears the tier-2 layer and the positional hull-stress layer before the release: "worse, worse, worse, release". Audio alone cannot resolve it. The review's recommendation: reword the anchor to the true timeline, and evaluate `TIER2_TICKS` 900 (inside Attention's safe range) in a playtest; **reject** freezing the tier during a hold (it would leak an indicator that the player is being forgiven; SUPERSEDED by the ruling below). A manual check is added (AC-AA-12 now covers testers who go still at the first knock). Previous default, superseded by the ruling: nothing changed; `TIER2_TICKS` is Attention's constant, so this is also a cross-GDD note **Resolved 2026-10-27 — user ruling (design-review decisions), a creative-director call under coordination rule 3:** Attention option A, escalation freezes while `L < theta_HOLD` (behaviour-keyed); the audio tiers follow the frozen escalation; the anchor is reworded to the true timeline; the review's "reject freezing" recommendation is superseded; Attention redoes its F-12 floor proof (cross-GDD note; D.5 now covers tier-1 bed states at breach entry; AC-AA-12 and AC-AA-150). *(Amended 2026-10-27 — user rulings)* | user (with the Attention owner and creative-director) | the first playtest of the tell |
